## Corpus Check
- 7201 files · ~8,961,080 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 56078 nodes · 115618 edges · 4083 communities (2330 shown, 1753 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7813 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `bd8091e5`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
